Heraclitean Extended Relational Field Dynamics: Filament Ontology,
Emergent Curvature, Bridge Maps to Effective Geometry and Quantum
Sectors, and the Emergent Origin of the Planck Scales
By Stephen Andreu

Physics,Theoretical Physics,Quantum Physics,Dynamical Systems,Quantum Gravity
This paper presents Heraclitean Extended Relational Field Dynamics (HERFD),  a speculative but mathematically structured research
program in which physical  spacetime is not fundamental and the primitive entities are interacting ﬁlaments  carrying internal ﬁelds,
director frames, and topological linking structure. The  framework rests on ﬁve conceptual postulates: no physical spacetime manifold is 
assumed microscopically, though representative conﬁgurations may be written in  an auxiliary similarity arena; the ontology is
